# hakuX-lr compatibility

Results apply only to the listed configuration. Stages: Boot, Intro, Menu, In-game, Playable. Outcomes: Completed, Crash, Timeout, Needs manual input. A timeout alone does not establish incompatibility. Playable means no observed problem prevents play during a short run; it does not cover a full playthrough.

- **amd64 Linux:** Boot 17, Intro 17, Menu 174, In-game 14, Playable 479
- **aarch64 Linux:** Untested 701
- **Android:** Untested 701

## Notes per game

- **007 - Agent Under Fire** (amd64 Linux, Playable · Completed): graphics: MGM logo, mission select, letterboxed helicopter cutscene and first-person gameplay with HUD render correctly, 30 fps.; audio: sound from 1.0 s, audible in 96 % of the run, loudest -23.8 dBFS RMS; Relaunched into BOND.XBE; after the opening helicopter cutscene (50-130 s), controlling Bond in first person with the Q-Decryptor and HUD at 150 s.
- **007 - Everything or Nothing** (amd64 Linux, Playable · Completed): graphics: THX logo, letterboxed intro cutscenes, the training level with HUD and the pause menu render correctly; the pause menu background is a near-white blur and its Objectives list under 'Primary' is empty (unconfirmed fault).; audio: sound from 7.5 s, audible in 75 % of the run, loudest -9.1 dBFS RMS; Reached gameplay at about 150 s: Bond in the basic-combat training with HUD ('Basic combat, step 1'); the final shot is the pause menu. The judge's INGAME at 110 s was a letterboxed cutscene (sniper-scope view).
- **007 - From Russia with Love** (amd64 Linux, Playable · Completed): graphics: MGM logo, gun-barrel intro, opening movie and the first level with health, minimap and ammo HUD render correctly, 30 fps; at 170-199 s a slanted strip along the left edge shows the wall and floor shifted against the rest of the scene (unconfirmed fault).; audio: sound from 5.0 s, audible in 89 % of the run, loudest -11.0 dBFS RMS; 0.19.1 + #104: the release stayed black for the whole run with the commit failing in a loop (Timeout); this run plays the intro and opening movie from 30 s and reaches gameplay at about 170 s, Bond walking a red carpet past party tables with the HUD, to the final shot (199 s). It logs 4,290 refused commits in the first 5 s, stepping down 4 KiB at a time from 64 MiB (48,380 KiB free); none led to a crash or stall.
- **007 - Nightfire** (amd64 Linux, Playable · Completed): graphics: MGM logo and the Paris car sequence with the sniper-scope HUD render correctly.; audio: sound from 0.5 s, audible in 87 % of the run, loudest -19.8 dBFS RMS; Relaunched into driving.xbe; reached gameplay at about 30 s: aiming from the car in the opening chase ('Tutorial: Limited weapon control', 'Pull R to Fire'). Displayed 45.1 fps (min 41.9), matching the guest swap rate of 45/s.
- **100 Bullets** (amd64 Linux, Playable · Completed): graphics: Acclaim logo and the Load/Options/Stats menu render correctly.; audio: sound from 2.5 s, audible in 14 % of the run, loudest -12.5 dBFS RMS; Gameplay inferred, not seen: the Load/Options/Stats menu with 'Resume game' is on screen from 30 s to the end; the log shows the level e3-01-streets loading after the Start at 15.5 s and the menu files loading on the Start at 25.5 s, and later Start presses never resumed. Guest swap rate 1905/s against 59.8 fps displayed (the core's counter reports 1,600-2,000 fps).
- **187 - Ride or Die** (amd64 Linux, Menu · Needs manual input): graphics: Ubisoft logo, the profile list and the name-entry keyboard render correctly, 60 fps.; audio: sound from -0.0 s, audible in 90 % of the run, loudest -18.6 dBFS RMS; Stalls in Player Profile creation from 30 s to the end: A types letters ('NNNN') on the name keyboard, the adaptive B returns to the empty profile list and A starts a new name again; the name is confirmed with Y ('Validate text'), which the run never presses. A 4.5 s guest-idle hang at frame 610 (about 10 s in) recovered.
- **2002 FIFA World Cup** (amd64 Linux, Playable · Completed): graphics: Loading screens, stadium, players, the free-kick arrow and the match HUD render correctly.; audio: Not usable: recorded while several games ran in parallel, and the recording mixed their sound.; Reached a match (USA vs France) by 70 s: a free kick with the power bar at 110 s and open play at 10:12 match time in the final shot. A 17.5 s guest hang right after boot (from frame 5, JIT compiling per the log) recovers; it explains the 0.2 fps minimum.
- **25 to Life** (amd64 Linux, Playable · Completed): graphics: Eidos logo, profile and main menus, and the first level with HUD render correctly.; audio: sound from 5.0 s, audible in 78 % of the run, loudest -12.0 dBFS RMS; Created a profile and started single player; reached gameplay at about 70 s ('Fight your way to the warehouse!'). Displayed fps 53.6 (min 19.8); guest-idle hangs of 3.0 s (frame 34) and 1.8 s (frame 2789), both recovered.
- **4x4 Evo 2** (amd64 Linux, Menu · Completed): graphics: Sand intro, the contour track map and the race HUD behind it (truck, speedometer, compass) render correctly.; audio: Not usable: recorded while several games ran in parallel, and the recording mixed their sound.; A race loads by 30 s, but every shot from 30 s to 54 s is the same map screen ('Press START to return to the menu', 'RACE STARTS IN 1'); the A presses change nothing, Start was held back after the judge's verdict, and the race never visibly starts, so the judge's INGAME is not confirmed gameplay. Guest swap rate about 137 per second against 58 fps displayed.
- **50 Cent - Bulletproof** (amd64 Linux, Menu · Needs manual input): graphics: Vivendi Universal logo, the difficulty menu and the PDA-style name keyboard render correctly at 30 fps.; audio: sound from 6.5 s, audible in 15 % of the run, loudest -13.1 dBFS RMS; After 'Choose Difficulty' (30 s) it loops through profile creation to the end: A types 'a' on the name keyboard and the adaptive B returns to 'Create New Profile', whose A opens the keyboard again. It needs the cursor moved from 'a' to Done (left column) then A, or X ('Continue without saving') on the Create New Profile screen. A 4.0 s hang at frame 65 (JIT compiling) recovered.
- **Advent Rising** (amd64 Linux, Menu · Completed): graphics: Majesco logo and the letterboxed opening movie render correctly; the 45 s frame is plain white.; audio: sound from 5.0 s, audible in 51 % of the run, loudest -18.1 dBFS RMS; A save slot was written and the game relaunched itself (log: 'relaunch into default.xbe'); from about 50 s to the end the opening movie (level01sectiona.bik, with credits) plays, so no gameplay was reached. Hangs of 5.0 s at startup and 3.0 s after the relaunch (JIT compiling) both recovered.
- **Aeon Flux** (amd64 Linux, Playable · Completed): graphics: Animated intro clip, an in-engine cutscene and the first level with the health HUD and control hints render correctly, 30 fps.; audio: sound from -0.0 s, audible in 81 % of the run, loudest -14.0 dBFS RMS; 0.19.1 + #104: the release crashed before the first frame (KeBugCheck after an access violation); this run reaches gameplay at about 45 s, Aeon at a doorway with the health HUD and 'L Move / R Look' hints, to the final shot (79 s). The 61 refused commits in the first 5 s (the first 256 MiB with 53,800 KiB free, then halving, in three rounds) led to no crash or stall. A 0.9 s guest-idle hang at frame 348 recovered.
- **Aggressive Inline** (amd64 Linux, Playable · Completed): graphics: Z-Axis logo, level select and the first level with HUD render correctly, 60 fps.; audio: sound from 9.5 s, audible in 70 % of the run, loudest -18.5 dBFS RMS; Reached gameplay at about 50 s: skating in the tutorial level.
- **AirForce Delta Storm** (amd64 Linux, Playable · Completed): graphics: Title screen and the first mission with HUD render correctly, 60 fps.; audio: sound from 7.5 s, audible in 83 % of the run, loudest -21.9 dBFS RMS; Reached gameplay at about 30 s: flying the first mission with HUD and the mission timer running; the next Start paused it ('PAUSE - Please press START to begin') and the run ended there. The log has 23,135 NtReleaseSemaphore calls on handle 0 returning c0000008.
- **Alias** (amd64 Linux, Playable · Completed): graphics: Acclaim logo, the casino level and the PDA-style pause menu render correctly.; audio: sound from 11.5 s, audible in 34 % of the run, loudest -28.3 dBFS RMS; Reached gameplay at about 30 s: Sydney in the casino with the HUD meter; the Start presses then opened the pause menu (45 s: 'Checking the Xbox hard disk' from Load). Displayed fps 56.5 (min 29.3).
- **Aliens Versus Predator - Extinction** (amd64 Linux, Playable · Completed): graphics: EA logo, the profile screen and the top-down battlefield with units and HUD render correctly; the minimap is black apart from two markers (not confirmed whether that is fog of war).; audio: sound from 5.5 s, audible in 45 % of the run, loudest -27.1 dBFS RMS; Reached gameplay at about 50 s: a Marine squad (Comm Tech selected) on the first map. Displayed fps 25.2 median (min 8.7) against a guest swap rate of 31.1/s; a 2.9 s hang at frame 453 (JIT compiling) recovered.
- **All-Star Baseball 2003 featuring Derek Jeter** (amd64 Linux, Playable · Completed): graphics: Title screen, Controller Select, Pacific Bell Park, players, pitch HUD and pause menus render correctly.; audio: Not usable: recorded while several games ran in parallel, and the recording mixed their sound.; A Mariners at Giants game was running by 30 s (batter intro), and the pitch HUD with a live at-bat is seen at 150 s; otherwise the Start/A cycle keeps opening the pause menu, Controller Select and Audio Options, so the judge never got two gameplay frames in a row. Ran the full 300 s.

- **Jade Empire** (amd64 Linux, Boot · Crash): graphics: Black for the whole run; no frame was drawn.; audio: silent for the whole run (recorded); Requires an installed Xbox dashboard. default.xbe compares Y:\xboxdash.xbe and Y:\XODash\xonlinedash.xbe with D:\dashupdate.xbe, then launches the updater because the dashboard is absent. The updater cannot open HGGothicM.xtf under y:\fonts\*.xtf, faults at 0002ffd0, and reports 'Rebooting...' through DashUnhandledExceptionFilter within one second. This appears to be missing dashboard content rather than an emulation fault. Copying your own dashboard to hdd/Partition2 should let it continue; the disc's content was not tested.

The stall from the earlier run still shows. Issue: hakuX-lr #103 (https://github.com/portare-ch/hakuX-lr/issues/103).
